# 0.5 Complete PMS Master Table of Contents

## 0.5.1 Part 0 — Master Index & Governance

[Volume 0](VOLUME-00-MASTER-INDEX-AND-GOVERNANCE.md) establishes document control, executive definition, terminology, portfolio, hierarchy, registries, IDs, decisions, dependencies and roadmap.

## 0.5.1.1 Authored volume navigation

- [Volume 1 — Business and Commercial Foundation](VOLUME-01-BUSINESS-AND-COMMERCIAL-FOUNDATION.md): strategy, market methodology, twelve-vendor comparison, personas, business model, revenue, pricing, KPIs and commercial requirements.
- [Volume 2 — Global Product and Functional Architecture](VOLUME-02-GLOBAL-PRODUCT-AND-FUNCTIONAL-ARCHITECTURE.md): authority, C4 context/containers, technology rationale, polyrepo, domains, execution, tenancy, shared capabilities and journeys.

- [Volume 3 — Marketing Site](VOLUME-03-MARKETING-SITE.md): public screens, authorization, data, API/events, integrations, publishing, privacy and operations.
- [Marketing Site feature catalog](VOLUME-03-MARKETING-FEATURES.md): full field records for all 21 registered modules.

## 0.5.2 Master subject hierarchy

Every numbered source subject is reserved below. S identifiers retain source numbering, independent of the sequential volume plan in Volume 0. Entries are planned coverage; no entry means that a detailed chapter is complete. Governance/method subjects S56–S70 constrain all product volumes.
